  done answers `{bytes, duration, width, height, format}`; `GET /slideshow/:id/file` → `image/webp` or `video/mp4`; new `GET
  /slideshow/:id/poster` → `image/jpeg` (the first frame; webp and mp4). Output ≤ 25 MB for webp (`error.webp.too_large`).
- **Result row**: as 18.5 with `r2_key originals/<sid>-s<job>.webp`, `content_type image/webp`, `made_spec` = the plan including
  `format`, `quality`, `width`; its poster is the helper's poster JPEG stored as 13.3's public unguessable JPEG (no poster job).
- **Replace (owner-approved 2026-10-07, `apple/CONTRACT-GALLERY.md` R8)**: a post holds one slideshow per `format`. When the new row
  is stored, any other live `role 'slideshow'` row of the post with the same `made_spec.format` (absent = `"mp4"`, so rows made
  before 18.10 count as mp4) is deleted as 18.4 (R2 object, mirror, unshared poster, purge). The render's done answer lists it in
  `replaced: ["<id>"]`. This also changes 18.5's mp4 slideshows, which accumulated.
  `GET /studio/<sid>/render/<job>` done adds `format`. Library `v=3` lists it with `role 'slideshow'` and its `content_type`.

### 18.11 The gallery image: `POST /studio/<sid>/gallery-image` (keyed; lane S1)

- **Body** (≤ 2 KB): `{"items":[…], "layout":"strip"|"grid2"|"grid3"|"row", "queue":true, "priority":"focused"|null,
  "notify":true|false}`. `items`: 2-20 unique `item_index` values of live `item` rows of the post, in the order to draw, every one a
  photo (`image/*` other than `image/gif`; a video or gif → `400 error.webp.invalid_params`); fewer than 2 live photo items in the
  post → `409 error.studio.too_few_photos`; malformed → `400 error.webp.invalid_params`; `priority` without `queue` → 400; an
  expired session → 410.
- **Line**: a `LineEntry` (17.2) of `kind: "gallery_image"`, class 0 with `priority: "focused"`, else 1; a `studio_renders` row
  `kind 'gallery_image'`, `plan` = the body's items and layout, `status 'pending'`. Answer `202 {status:"pending", job, queued,
  queue_ahead}`; progress on `GET /studio/<sid>/render/<job>` with `phase` `queued` | `uploading` | `composing`; done
  `{status:"success", url?, item_id, bytes, width, height, cropped:[index…], upscaled:[index…]}`.
- **Helper**: `PUT /gallery/:id/inputs/:n` (n 0-19; ≤ 200 MB each, ≤ 500 MB a job; holds the helper like `/slideshow`, 5 min idle
  reap, 429 busy), `POST /gallery/:id/start {layout, slides:[{n}]}` (the helper probes the sizes and runs
  `apple/CONTRACT-GALLERY.md` 6.3-6.4) → 202 | 400 | 409 (an input missing); `GET /gallery/:id` → pending `{phase:"composing"}` |
  done `{bytes, width, height, cropped:[n…], upscaled:[n…]}` | error; `GET /gallery/:id/file` → `image/jpeg`; `DELETE` → 204.
  Errors: `error.webp.invalid_params` (an input that is not a still), `error.webp.encode_failed`, `error.webp.too_large` (> 50 MB),
  `error.webp.timeout` (10 min).
- **Result row**: `originals/<sid>-g<job>.jpg`, `kind 'private'`, `source 'studio'`, `role 'export'`, `made_from` = the item ids,
  `made_spec` = `{"kind":"gallery","layout":"grid3","items":[…]}` (≤ 512 bytes), `post_key`, width/height, the post's visibility
  (its lead's), a poster job (image). **Replace** (R8): another live `export` row of the post with `made_spec.kind "gallery"` and the
  same `layout` is deleted (as 18.4) after the new row is stored, listed in the done answer's `replaced`; other layouts stay. `notify` as 9.3 (`rendered` copy:
  `gallery image ready · <w>×<h> · <size>`).

### 18.12 The share sheet's one request: `slideshow.format` and `gallery_image` on `POST /studio` (lane S1)

- `POST /studio` (with `items`) takes `slideshow` as 18.2 plus 18.10's `format`/`quality`/`width`, **or** `gallery_image: {items,
  layout}` (18.11's fields), not both (400). `slideshow.seconds` carries `null` for every video and gif item (its own length) and the
  photo time for every photo, in the plan's order (the approved boards' shape); a number for a video or `null` for a photo → 400 on `POST /studio/<sid>/slideshow`; on `POST /studio` the item types are not known until the save is done, so the mix is checked then and the make ends `error.webp.invalid_params` (the save stays). The make's render row is created with the session (its job id is in the answer as
  `make: {job, kind}`) and joins the line (class 1) when the save is ready, over the asked items **that were saved** (a failed item is
  dropped from the plan; for a gallery image, fewer than 2 photos left → the render ends `error.studio.too_few_photos`, the save
  stays; for a slideshow, fewer than 2 items left → `error.studio.not_gallery`).
- **One message.** With a make, the client sends `notify: {on: ["rendered", "failed"], label}`: exactly one Hark message when the
  make ends (`<label> · slideshow webp ready` / `<label> · slideshow ready` / `<label> · gallery image ready`, with the 9.4 size and
  URL line), or when the save fails (9.4's save failure), or when the save succeeds and the make fails (`<label> · saved <n> items.
  <what> couldn't be made — <reason>`; for a webp over 60 s: `<label> · saved <n> items. the slideshow webp would be <m:ss> and
  webps stop at 60 s. open cobalt to make the mp4.`). `saved` is not sent unless asked. The message's URL opens the session.
- Bodies the share sheet sends (pinned for lane A4; `public` and `label` as today's instant share):
  `{"url", "items":"all", "item_count":10, "public":true, "origin":"share", "queue":true, "notify":{"on":["saved","failed"],"label"}}`
  (save all); the same plus `"slideshow":{"items":[0,…,9], "seconds":[2,…,null], "fade":true, "frame":"keep", "sound":"none",
  "format":"webp", "quality":"med", "width":480}` and `notify.on: ["rendered","failed"]`; or plus `"gallery_image":{"items":[photo
  indices], "layout":"grid3"}` and `notify.on: ["rendered","failed"]`.

### 18.13 Renders of one item: `item` on `POST /studio/<sid>/render` (lane S1)

- Optional integer `item`: the `item_index` of a live `item` row of the session whose type is a video or gif; the render reads that
  row's `r2_key` instead of the session's lead. A photo item → `409 error.studio.not_video`; an unknown or deleted index → `404
  error.studio.not_found`; on a session without items → 400 `error.webp.invalid_params`. Absent = the lead (today). The webp is
  stored as today (public `media` bucket) and listed in `v=3` with `made_from` = that item's id.

**Capability (18.10-18.13):** `features.gallery_make: true` when the running helper answers `x-cobalt-helper: gallery=1,make=1`
(checked as `gallery=1` is, `studio.ts:812-836`); without it the app hides the three makes and the share sheet draws only `save all`.
New code: `error.studio.too_few_photos`. Reused: `error.studio.not_video`, `error.studio.not_gallery`, `error.webp.*` as above.

### 18.14 The webp of one photo: a still made animated so Discord offers "favourite" (added 2026-10-21; owner: "convert jpg still images to animated webp so people can favourite on discord")

Discord offers "favourite" only on animated files. img2webp cannot write one from a single picture: WebPAnimEncoder merges
identical frames and writes a lone frame as a plain still (`VP8 `, no `VP8X`/`ANIM`; checked with libwebp 1.3.2).

- **Route**: `POST /studio/<sid>/slideshow` (18.5, 18.10) with `format: "webp"` and **one** item: `items: [i]`, `seconds: [s]`
  (0.5-15). The item must be a photo (`image/*` other than gif; HEIC `400 error.studio.unsupported_image` as before); a video or
  gif alone → `400 error.webp.invalid_params` (that is a render). `width` may be **640** here (320 | 480 | 640; 2+ items keep
  320 | 480). The mp4 and a plan chained to a save (18.12) still need 2 items.
- **Which photo**: a gallery's photo item by `item_index`; a session with no item rows whose original is a photo (a single
  saved photo, an upload, a reopened library photo) has that photo as item 0, or as its own `item_index` when it is a
  reopened photo of a gallery (so the client sends the same index either way).
- **Reopening**: `POST /library/items/<id>/studio` (5d) takes a private `image/jpeg|png|webp` original too and opens a photo
  session (probed like an upload); HEIC stays `400 error.studio.not_video`. Internally the adopt body carries `photo: true`; the
  web Worker's `/library/adopt` sends no such field and still refuses photos. `POST /studio/<sid>/render` on a photo session
  (no `item`) → `409 error.studio.not_video`.
- **Helper**: one slide is allowed (it always was, 1-20); `width` 640 only with one slide. After img2webp, a webp with fewer than
  2 frames is rewritten (`animateStill`, `helper/lib.js`) as `VP8X` (animation, + alpha when the picture has it) + `ANIM`
  (loop forever) + 2 `ANMF` frames that each carry the same bitstream (no re-encode), the plan's time split between them. The
  helper says `still=1` in `x-cobalt-helper`; a one-photo make is never started on a helper without it (`error.webp.unavailable`).
- **Result row**: as 18.10 (`role 'slideshow'`, `image/webp`, the helper's poster), named `<title>-animated.webp`,
  `made_spec` gains `"still": true`. `post_key` is the post the session's original belongs to: the session id, or `<id>` of a
  session whose link is `upload:<id>` (a reopened photo's webp joins the original post, as GET /library groups it).
- **Replace (R8)**: a still replaces only an earlier still of the **same photo** (same `made_spec.items[0]`); a slideshow webp
  replaces only a slideshow webp. The mp4 rule is unchanged.
- **Capability**: `features.still_webp: true` while `gallery_make` and the running helper said `still=1`.

---

## 19. Direct media links: a pasted link to a file just saves (addendum, built 2026-10-10; owner: pasting a Discord CDN attachment answered `error.api.link.invalid`)

A link to a media file on a host cobalt has no service for (`https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/<id>/<id>/LiaPoor.png?ex=…&is=…&hm=…`) is
saved as that file. **No new route, no new request field, no migration, no client change**: `POST /studio` with such a URL (and
`items`, `public`, `notify`, `origin`, `queue` as ever) now works where it used to end `error.api.link.invalid`. `GET /capabilities` gains
`features.direct_links: true` (missing = `false`), true only while the running helper says `direct=1` in its `x-cobalt-helper` header
(now `gallery=1,make=1,direct=1`; the Durable Object keeps it next to `gallery` and `make`, `helper:caps`). An app uses the flag to skip
its own "not a supported link" pre-check; `POST /` (upstream cobalt) is unchanged and still answers 400 for such a link.

### 19.1 The rule (helper `POST /fetch`, `helper/server.js` `runFetch`)

1. The helper asks cobalt as always. Only when cobalt answers **`error.api.link.invalid` or `error.api.link.unsupported`** (no other
   code, no other route: `POST /jobs` keeps its behaviour) is the submitted URL tried as the file itself.
2. The URL must pass the public-URL rule (19.2). Otherwise: cobalt's own error, as before.
3. It is downloaded with the studio's usual caps (200 MB per item, the 240 s fetch budget) and typed **by its bytes**: a still (jpeg png
   webp heic) or a GIF by `sniffType`; a video only when its container is one the studio stores (`sniffVideo`: mp4 / mov / m4v by a video
   `ftyp` brand, webm / mkv by EBML) **and** ffmpeg finds a video stream. **The first 64 bytes decide early**: a body that cannot be an
   accepted file is cut at its first chunk (`downloadToFile` `acceptHead`), so a 200 MB html page or mp3 is never fetched. A HEIC needs
   more than its magic: a plausible `ftyp` box (16-128 bytes, a HEIF brand) **and** an `ispe` box inside `meta > iprp > ipco`.
   **Pixel size from the header, before any decoder** (`imageDimensions`: PNG IHDR, JPEG SOF, GIF logical screen, WebP VP8/VP8L/VP8X,
   HEIC `ispe`): a still or GIF over **64 MP** (the helper's save cap, `maxSavePixels`) is `error.studio.too_large`, a direct link whose header does not
   say its size is refused. This applies to **every** still the fetch saves (picker and gallery photos too, per item), because a 797 KB
   16000x16000 PNG cost ~950 MB to thumbnail in the 1 GiB container. Note this refuses a 48 MP phone photo (8064x6048); raise
   `maxSavePixels` if that matters (~3.7 bytes per pixel to thumbnail). The content-type header and the file name decide nothing; the
   extension of a saved video comes from the bytes. html, json, audio (mp3, m4a), an audio-only mp4, an unknown container, an empty
   body, a 4xx/5xx: **cobalt's original error** (`error.api.link.invalid` / `.unsupported`).
4. The exception: a body over the cap is **`error.studio.too_large`** (the user should learn the file is real and too big).
5. The result is one file, exactly the answer of a single-photo / single-video save (no `items`, `picker_count: null`): the same session and
   `media_items` rows, thumb → poster for a still, posters for a video, `public` hosting, `notify`, the line. `title` = the URL's file name
   without extension (`LiaPoor.png` → `LiaPoor`; none → null; control, zero-width and bidi override/isolate characters removed). `service` is what the session already records for every link
   (`serviceFromUrl`: `discordapp`); the helper's answer says the same, and adds `direct: true`. **The signed query is kept nowhere past the
   save** (it is a credential): the helper gets the full link while the save runs; when the save becomes ready the Durable Object stores the
   link **without query, fragment or userinfo** in `studio_sessions.link` (same statement as `ready`), in `media_items.link`, and in
   both R2 objects' `customMetadata.source` (the private original and the public copy hosted from it; a mirror made later is
   made from the bare row). A save that **fails** with `error.api.link.invalid` / `.unsupported` (cobalt never knew the link: it is not a
   post) has its session link cut the same way. Links of supported services (posts, whose query can matter) and every other failure keep
   the link as before.
6. `items: "all"` / `"first-video"` on such a link is the one file; an `items` list naming an index other than 0, or an `item_count`
   other than 1, ends with cobalt's original error.

### 19.2 The public-URL rule (`helper/lib.js`: `checkPublicUrl`, `isPublicIp`, `makeSafeFetch`)

- **Scheme/credentials/port**: `http` or `https` only; **no `user:pass@`**; **port 80 or 443 only** (whichever scheme; the number decides). A
  link to `host:8080`, `:6379`, `:9000` is refused: nothing a person pastes to save a photo lives there, and every other port is an
  internal service.
- **Name/address**: an IP literal must be a public global-unicast address; a name must contain a dot and not be a private-looking
  spelling (`localhost`, `*.localhost`, `*.local`, `*.internal`, a trailing dot ignored). Refused ranges: `0/8`, `10/8`, `100.64/10`,
  `127/8`, `169.254/16` (the metadata address), `172.16/12`, `192.168/16`, `192.0.0/24`, `192.0.2/24`, `198.18/15`, `198.51.100/24`,
  `203.0.113/24`, `224/4` and up; IPv6 is a **whitelist** (only `2000::/3`; never `::`, `::1`, `fc00::/7`, `fe80::/10`, `ff00::/8`,
  Teredo, documentation), and a v4 address written inside a v6 one (`::ffff:a.b.c.d` in either spelling, `::a.b.c.d`, NAT64
  `64:ff9b::/96`, 6to4 `2002::/16`) is judged by the v4 address inside. URL parsing already turns decimal / hex / octal IPv4 into dotted form.
- **Resolution is the connection's own**: the name is resolved by the `lookup` of the socket that connects, and **every** address it
  returns must be public (one private address among public ones refuses the host). The address checked is the address connected to, so
  a rebinding name gets no second lookup to flip; nothing is resolved by a separate pre-check.
- **Redirects** are followed by hand, **at most 3**: each `Location` goes through the same URL rule and a fresh guarded connection; a
  4th redirect, a loop, an unparseable `Location`, a `file:` / `ftp:` target, a private target, and an `https` → `http` downgrade are refusals
  (`error.webp.bad_source` inside the helper; cobalt's original error to the client). The next hop is never requested after a refusal.
- **Nothing of the client's travels**: the remote host gets `user-agent`, `accept`, `accept-encoding: identity` (the byte caps count what is on
  the wire), `connection: close`, and nothing else: no `cookie`, `authorization`, `referer`, no internal key, no API key. (The Worker
  already strips the client's headers; the helper never had them.)
- **Caps**: the body is counted **while streaming** (content-length is only an early out) and the socket is destroyed at the cap. The
  30 s idle timer covers an established socket (waiting for the response, or between chunks); DNS and connecting are bounded only by the
  job's 240 s signal. A socket that closes without any response (including a `101 Switching Protocols`, which is refused) or answers a
  status `Response` cannot represent (600-999) is a failed download, settled at once.
- **Tunnel exemption**: only cobalt's own `GET /tunnel` on its loopback origin (the exact path) is fetched plainly; any other path on
  that origin goes through the same public-only fetch (and is refused).
- **Logs**: a link's query is a credential (a signed token). The helper logs `host/path` only (`logUrl`: no userinfo, no query), never the
  URL; the Worker's request log (`request_log.url_prefix`) is now cut at the first `?` or `#` and loses any `user:pass@` before its
  80-character slice (this also covers every other link).

### 19.3 Picker and redirect items are hardened the same way

Before this change a picker / redirect item URL from cobalt was fetched with a plain `fetch` that followed redirects anywhere and trusted
the name. They share `downloadToFile`, so the helper now gives every download that is **not cobalt's own `/tunnel`** (the container's
loopback, by design) the same `makeSafeFetch`: ports 80/443, resolved-address check, at most 3 hops each re-checked, no credentials. A
refused item is `error.webp.bad_source` (per item in a gallery, as any failed item). WebP jobs (`POST /jobs`) get it too.

### 19.4 Files and tests

`helper/lib.js` (`isPublicIp`, `checkPublicUrl`, `makeSafeFetch`, `sniffVideo`, `filenameFromUrl`, `logUrl`; `downloadToFile` re-throws a
refusal), `helper/server.js` (`HELPER_CAPS`, `runFetch`, `fetchOne` `direct`, the default `doDownload`, `fetchPolicy` for tests),
`src/studio.ts` (`helperDirect`, `helper:caps`, `finalize` and `markError` strip the link), `src/library.ts` (`stripLinkSecrets`),
`src/worker.ts` (`helperHas`, log prefix), `src/app-routes.ts` (`direct_links`).
`test/direct-links.test.ts` (the rule: address and URL tables, `makeSafeFetch` against a real local origin with a fake resolver for
redirects, private and rebinding destinations, credentials, ports, mid-stream cap, timeouts, headers; `POST /fetch` through the real
helper for image / gif / video / html / json / audio / oversize / chain > 3 / private redirect / log hygiene / picker hardening; one real
ffmpeg describe) and `test/direct-links-api.test.ts` (through the Worker and the Durable Object with the fake helper: single-item
rows, poster, public, library listing, notify, request-log prefix, capability).

### 19.5 Deploy (owner; not run by the lane)

Container image (the helper) and Worker. No migration. Either order is safe: a Worker ahead of the image advertises `direct_links: false`
until the new helper answers once; an image ahead of the Worker just works for old clients.

### 19.6 Old Discord links: refreshed through Discord's API (addendum, built 2026-10-27; owner: saving `https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/911068860487192596/1505800458986590279/Big_Reveal.gif` failed)

Discord signs every attachment link (`?ex=<expiry>&is=<issued>&hm=<hmac>`) and answers **404 to an unsigned or expired one, from
anywhere**, so a link copied without its query (or older than about a day) failed with `error.api.link.invalid`. With a Discord
**bot token** the helper asks Discord to sign the link again and saves that. **No new route, request field, migration or client
change.** Without the token everything stays exactly as in 19.1.

- **Secret**: `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` (Worker secret from the secrets file, like `COBALT_API_KEY`). The Durable Object puts it into the
  container's env **only when it is non-empty** (so the container env is unchanged without it); the supervisor hands it to the helper
  and keeps it out of cobalt's own child process. It is never logged. The bot needs no server, no intents and no permissions.
- **Which links**: a section 19 direct link (cobalt answered `error.api.link.invalid` / `.unsupported` and the URL passed 19.2) whose
  host is `cdn.discordapp.com` or `media.discordapp.net` and whose path is `/attachments/<digits>/<digits>/<name>` (or
  `/ephemeral-attachments/...`). Avatars, emojis, other hosts and every service link are never sent to Discord.
- **When** (`helper/server.js` `runFetch`): a link **without** all of `ex`, `is`, `hm` is refreshed **before** the download (Discord would
  refuse it anyway). A signed link is downloaded as it is; only when that download answers **401, 403, 404 or 410** is it refreshed and
  downloaded **once** more. At most one refresh per save.
- **The call** (`helper/lib.js` `refreshDiscordUrl`): `POST https://discord.com/api/v10/attachments/refresh-urls`,
  `Authorization: Bot <token>`, body `{"attachment_urls": ["<the link without ex/is/hm>"]}` (other query parameters kept), answer
  `{"refreshed_urls": [{"original": "...", "refreshed": "<signed link>"}]}`, 10 s budget. Not in Discord's public docs; shape confirmed
  from the community reference (docs.discord.food, "Refresh Attachment URLs": 1-50 URLs, unsigned URLs accepted) and existing bot code
  (`refreshAttachmentURLs([url]).refreshed_urls[0].refreshed`). `refreshed` is used only when it is a URL on one of the two CDN hosts
  with **the same attachment path** and no userinfo, and passes the public-URL rule (19.2); then the normal download with every 19.1
  cap. Anything else (no token, a non-2xx answer such as 401 for a bad token, junk, a network failure) means no refresh: the link is
  tried as it is and fails as before.
- **Result**: unchanged from 19.1. Still refused after the refresh (deleted message, file gone): cobalt's original error. The
  `title` is the file name of the link the owner pasted; the stored link is that link without query (19.1.5), so neither the old nor
  the new signature reaches D1, R2 or a log line (the log says `discord link refreshed <host/path>`).
- **Capability**: the helper's `x-cobalt-helper` header adds `discord=1` while it has a token (`gallery=1,make=1,direct=1,still=1,discord=1`);
  the Durable Object keeps it in `helper:caps` next to the others, and `GET /capabilities` has **`features.discord_refresh`** (missing =
  `false`), true only while the running helper said both `direct=1` and `discord=1`. Apps need not read it: the save just works.
- **Files and tests**: `helper/lib.js` (`isDiscordAttachment`, `hasDiscordSignature`, `refreshDiscordUrl`, `DISCORD_REFUSED`;
  `downloadToFile`'s refusal error carries `status`), `helper/server.js` (`refreshDiscord`, `runFetch`, `HELPER_CAPS_DISCORD`),
  `helper/supervisor.js`, `src/index.ts` (envVars), `src/studio.ts` (`helper:caps`), `src/worker.ts` / `src/app-routes.ts`
  (`discord_refresh`), `cloudflare.config.ts` (secret). `test/discord-refresh.test.ts` (the real helper against a local CDN + API:
  refreshed, still refused, bad token, unusable answers, no token, non-Discord, log hygiene) and `test/direct-links-api.test.ts`
  (the capability).
- **Deploy (owner)**: add `"DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN"` to the secrets file (an empty string is allowed and keeps it off; `cf deploy` refuses
  while a declared secret is missing), then one API deploy (Worker and image together). No migration.

## 20. 7TV emotes

Keyed. The Worker only: it calls 7TV and writes D1 + the public media bucket. The container is not woken. The phone and the keyboard do not call 7TV.

7TV's search is `POST https://7tv.io/v4/gql` (`emotes { search }` and `emotes { emote }`), sorted `TOP_ALL_TIME` descending. A live emote (catJAM, checked 2026-10-14) has webp at scales 1, 2, 3 and 4, plus a still `*_static.webp` and gif/avif copies. The file we keep is the webp with the greatest `scale` whose `frameCount` is greater than 1 when the emote is animated. Static fallbacks are not saved. A file over 15 MB steps down to the next scale. The grid preview is scale 2 when that webp exists, else 1, then 3, then 4. Zero-width and private emotes are left out. The download URL's host must be `cdn.7tv.app`.

`GET /emotes?q=` — `q` is 1..64 trimmed characters, else 400 `error.emote.query`. 200:

```
{ "items": [ { "id", "name", "animated", "preview_url", "file_url", "library_id", "library_url" } ] }
```

`preview_url` is the grid picture. `file_url` is the largest webp, the one a copy or a save keeps. `library_id` is our row id when this emote is already saved, else null, and `library_url` is that file's public URL or null. At most 24. 7TV not answering is 502 `error.emote.unavailable`. Nothing is written.

`POST /emotes` body `{ "id" }` — id is 20..32 letters or digits, else 400 `error.emote.query`. Unknown, deleted, zero-width, private, or no usable webp: 404 `error.emote.not_found`. The bytes are a public object `<10 base62>.webp` on `media.capybaraharmony.com`, same names as a made webp. The `media_items` row is `source: "emote"`, `kind: "public"`, `bucket: "media"`, `content_type: "image/webp"`, `name` the emote's name, `link` the token `7tv:<id>` (not a 7tv.app URL). No new column. Saving the same id again is 200 and the same row, and does not download again. The first save is 201 and the library file shape plus `emote_id`.

`GET /library?v=3` is unchanged. This row is not an original (`saved` / `upload`), so the post's kind stays `webp`. Clients tell an emote by `source == "emote"`. Removing it is the existing `DELETE /library/items/<id>/post` (a role-less file is not deletable on `DELETE /library/items/<id>`).

No migration. Deploy the API Worker only. The app decodes each library file on its own, so an old build drops a file whose `source` is `emote` and can show an empty post. Ship the app that accepts the word before relying on a saved emote.

### 20.1 Top, trending, new and the global set (addendum, 2026-10-30; owner: "I cannot search by top global or filter by weekly daily popular")

`GET /emotes` also takes `sort` and `page`. With no `sort` the route is exactly as above (a name is required, top of all
time), so old clients are unchanged.

- `sort`: `top` (most used of all time), `top_daily`, `top_weekly`, `top_monthly` (most used over that window),
  `trending_daily`, `trending_weekly`, `trending_monthly` (rising fastest), `new` (newest uploads), `global` (7TV's global
  emote set). Anything else is 400 `error.emote.query`.
- With a `sort`, `q` is optional (0..64 trimmed characters): empty browses the whole list, a name narrows it.
- `page`: 1..50, default 1, 24 a page. Anything else is 400 `error.emote.query`.
- 7TV calls (checked live 2026-10-30): the sorts are v4 `emotes.search(query, sort: { sortBy, order: DESCENDING })` with
  `sortBy` `TOP_ALL_TIME`, `TOP_DAILY`, `TOP_WEEKLY`, `TOP_MONTHLY`, `TRENDING_DAILY`, `TRENDING_WEEKLY`,
  `TRENDING_MONTHLY`, `UPLOAD_DATE`, and a null query browses; `global` is v4 `emoteSets.global.emotes(query, page,
  perPage)`. The same filters as above (no zero-width, private or deleted emotes; files on `cdn.7tv.app`) apply, so a page
  can hold fewer than 24.

200 (every answer of the route now carries the paging keys):

```
{ "items": [ { "id", "name", "animated", "preview_url", "file_url", "width", "height", "library_id", "library_url" } ],
  "sort": "trending_weekly", "page": 1, "has_more": true }
```

`width` and `height` are `file_url`'s size. `has_more` is true while 7TV has another page (capped at page 50).

The chat AI and MCP: `cobalt.emotes.find({ query?, sort?, page? })` (section 33.10) answers this body. A saved emote is a
library image (`source: "emote"`, webp, often animated), so anything that takes a library image file takes it.

Clients: the app's emote card (the 7tv circle) has chips `saved` · trending today/this week/this month · top today/this
week/this month/all time · new · global; `saved` is the old card (saved emotes, typing searches top of all time), a list
chip loads at once and typing narrows it, and the next page loads at the last tile. The web library has an emotes view with
the same chips (default trending this week) through the web Worker's `/api/library/emotes`.

No migration. Deploy the API Worker before the web Worker and the app.

## 21. Library search (addendum, built 2026-10-17; owner: "have a cloudflare worker embed our media for searching", hosted on a private Hugging Face Space, `capybaraharmony/cobalt-embed`)

One vector per library post, made by Google's EmbeddingGemma 2 (text and image encoders) on a private Hugging Face Space (`deploy/huggingface/embed`, its README has the Space contract), stored in D1 `media_embeddings` (migration `0010_search.sql`) and searched by the API Worker (`src/embed.ts`). A post is embedded from `title: <custom title, else the lead file's name> | text: <its file names · the link's host and path>` plus its poster when the poster is on `media.capybaraharmony.com`, so a query finds a post by the words in it or by what its poster shows. Vectors are 256 numbers (Matryoshka truncation, L2-normalized).

`GET /library/search?q=<1..200 chars>&limit=<1..50, default 20>&v=<2|3>` (keyed or service) → 200 `{ "status": "success", "query", "model": "google/embeddinggemma-2", "posts": [ <GET /library post, same v rules> + "score" ], "index": { "posts": <posts with a vector>, "pending": <posts whose vector is missing or out of date> } }`, best first. `score` is the cosine plus 0.3 when the query is found as plain text in the title or text (so a post found by its name ranks first, even before its vector exists). There is no score cut-off; clients show the order. Errors: 400 `error.library.bad_request` (empty or long query, bad limit); 503 `error.search.off` (no Space configured); 503 `error.search.waking` (only from callers that need meaning, such as stage pools; GET /library/search no longer sends it). When the Space cannot answer (asleep, booting, down), GET /library/search answers 200 with name matches instead and adds `"fallback": "text"`: posts holding the whole query as text first, then posts holding more of its words, newest first within a score (added 2026-10-23, so search never hangs on "waking up"; older clients without the key show them as results). A sleeping Space answers a POST at once without waking, so a call the Space could not answer wakes it with `GET /` and tries once more within its time (`spaceClient`, embed.ts); the 15-minute cron also sends `GET /` every run, so the Space never reaches its 48 h idle sleep; 502 `error.search.failed`; 503 `error.api.generic`.

`POST /library/search/index` (keyed) runs one indexer pass now and answers `{ "status": "success", "posts", "indexed", "failed", "removed", "pending", "unavailable" }`. With `?drain=1` it loops passes for up to 25 s and adds `"passes"` and `"stop"` (section 23.3); any other `drain` value is 400 `error.library.bad_request`. A pass embeds up to 8 posts whose inputs changed (newest first; a custom title or a new poster re-embeds), deletes the rows of posts that are gone, and calls the Space only when something needs a vector. The 15-minute cron drains: it loops passes until nothing is pending, the Space is unavailable or 10 minutes are up (section 22.3; about 96 posts a run at ~50 s a batch on the CPU Space, where one pass a run gave 8). A Space that is asleep, down or misconfigured holds nothing against any post; a post the Space refuses (4xx) is retried an hour later, three times, then left until its inputs change.

`GET /library/search/post?key=<post_key>` (keyed or service; built 2026-10-17, owner: "when i click on the post maybe some ui to show what indexes are") is one post's index status, for the post's detail screen (app) and the web library. `key` is the post's `id` from `GET /library` (1..2048 chars). → 200 `{ "status": "success", "post_key", "state", "search_enabled": <bool>, "model"?, "dims"?, "embedded_at"?: <ms>, "has_image"?: <bool>, "failed_at"?: <ms>, "retry_at"?: <ms>, "attempts": <int>, "input": { "title": <string|null>, "text": <string>, "image_url": <string|null> }, "embedded_matches_input": <bool> }`. `state`: `"indexed"` (a vector of the current input), `"stale"` (a vector of an older input: renamed or a new poster; the next pass re-embeds it, and search still uses the old vector), `"pending"` (no vector yet and nothing failed for this input), `"failed"` (the Space refused this input; `retry_at` = `failed_at` + 1 h, `attempts` < 3), `"gave_up"` (refused 3 times; waits until the input changes). `model`, `dims`, `embedded_at` and `has_image` (the Space saw the poster) are present only while a vector exists (a `"failed"` post may keep an older input's vector); `failed_at` only after a refusal; `attempts` counts refusals of the current input (0 once it changes). `input` is what would be embedded now (the poster only when on the media domain); `embedded_matches_input` is true exactly in `"indexed"`. The vector itself is never returned. It answers from D1 even while no Space is configured (`search_enabled: false`). Errors: 400 `error.library.bad_request` (missing or over 2048 chars); 404 `error.library.not_found` (not a live post: unknown or every file deleted); 503 `error.api.generic`. Other methods are 404.

`features.search` in `GET /capabilities` is true exactly while `EMBED_URL` (an https URL, a plain var) and the secret `EMBED_TOKEN` are set; `EMBED_KEY` (secret) is sent as `x-embed-key`. Apply migration 0010 before deploying.

`GET /library/map` (keyed or service; built 2026-10-18, owner: "is there a way of visualising the embedding ... constellation maps light and dark mode") hands over every embedded post's vector so a client can draw the library as a map (the web library's "sky map" lays them out with t-SNE in the browser). → 200 `{ "status": "success", "model", "dims", "search_enabled": <bool>, "posts": [{ "post_key", "title": <string|null>, "text", "image_url": <string|null>, "latest": <ms>, "v8": <base64> }], "unmapped": <int> }`, newest post first. `v8` is the vector as int8, scaled so its largest component is ±127 (only the direction matters for similarity). Only posts with a vector of the current model are listed (a stale vector still counts); `unmapped` is how many live posts have none yet. It answers from D1 even while no Space is configured. Errors: 503 `error.api.generic`. Other methods are 404.

**Speed (built 2026-10-31; owner: "how can we like fix the whole search to be lighting fast").** `GET /library/search` reads the library from D1 in parallel, and in parallel with the Space's query call, instead of one read after another. A query's vector is remembered by its exact text (this isolate's memory, at most 256, then the colo's Cache API for 30 days), so the same words never wait on the Space twice. Every search logs one `[search] timing` line (`embed`, `remembered`, `reads`, `search`, `posts`, `total` in ms, `fallback`), and a call that only succeeded after a wake-up logs `[search] space answered after a wake-up`.

`GET /library/search?warm=1` (keyed or service; no `q`, else 400 `error.library.bad_request`) is the warm-up a client sends at the first letter typed: it asks the Space for a one-word query (waking it if it sleeps) while D1 reads what a search reads, then answers 200 `{ "status": "success", "warm": { "space": "ready" | "unavailable" | "off", "ms" } }`. Fire and forget; nothing changes. The app sends it once per typed query that starts from an empty field, at most once a minute, and no longer pages the whole library while a server search is still out: it waits for the answer, then fills in name matches in the background.

## 22. Rich link pages (addendum, built 2026-10-17; owner: "hold pressing should just process and give me a url ... rich embedding compatibility like fixupx does it")

Every public file `https://media.capybaraharmony.com/<name>.<ext>` also has a page at `https://share.capybaraharmony.com/<name>` (`src/share.ts`). The page is plain HTML whose Open Graph and Twitter card tags point at the file, so the link unfurls in Discord, iMessage, X, Telegram, Slack and the like; someone who opens it sees the file on a black page. The API Worker answers the share host itself, before the key gate: no key, no CORS, the container is never woken.

- `GET|HEAD /<name>` (also `/<name>.<ext>` and a trailing slash). `<name>` is 6..40 letters or digits. The row is the live `media_items` row whose public object is `<name>.<ext>` (`r2_key` in the public bucket, else `public_key`) and whose effective visibility is public. Anything else (private, deleted, unknown, any other path) is the same 404, so a page says nothing about a private file. Other methods 405. D1 down 503.
- A video: `og:type video.other`, `og:video` / `og:video:secure_url` / `og:video:type` / width / height, `twitter:card player` with `twitter:player:stream`, and the server-made poster as `og:image` (only a poster on the media domain). An image or webp: `og:image` with its type and size, `twitter:card summary_large_image`. Title: the post's custom title, else the file's name without its extension. A video's `og:description` is its length (`0:10`).
- Headers: `cache-control: public, max-age=60` (a file made private loses its page within a minute; the Worker does not cache it at the edge), a CSP that allows only the media origin, `noindex`. `/robots.txt` disallows everything.

`GET /capabilities` gains top-level `share_base_url` (`https://share.capybaraharmony.com/`, or null while `SHARE_BASE_URL` is unset or not https). Every place a client hands out a public file's link uses `<share_base_url><name>` instead (owner, 2026-10-17: "I don't want to send the media links directly"): the app's copy buttons, share sheets with a link (not a file), the links it shows, the Live Activity's copy link, the keyboard's inserted links (`Settings.shareLink(for:)`, from the last capabilities), the web library and studio pages (`shareUrl()`, hard-coded bases like `DEFAULT_API`), and the Hark message of a finished webp (`NotifyDeps.shareLink`). Without it, the file's URL as before. Shortcuts outputs stay file URLs: a shortcut usually downloads them.

No migration. Deploy the API Worker: `share.capybaraharmony.com` is a new custom domain in `api/cloudflare.config.ts`, created by `cf deploy`.

## 23. Queue (addendum, built 2026-10-17; owner: one queue screen in the app for everything in flight)

The app's queue screen shows its own jobs (downloads, saves, uploads, renders it started) and the server's work that it cannot see
from its jobs: the line other callers fill, the search index and the posters. `GET /queue` is that server side in one call. Nothing
under `api/` changes; the container is never woken.

### 23.1 `GET /queue` (keyed)

Keyed like `GET /studio/line`: `Authorization: Api-Key <key>`. No key or a bad one: 401 `error.api.auth.key.invalid`; the
library-service credential and every other method: 404. `cache-control: no-store`. Always 200 once the key is valid: each part is
fetched on its own and is `null` when it fails.

```json
{ "status": "success", "now": 1790000000000,
  "line": { "status": "success", "now": 1790000000000, "running": null, "entries": [], "max": 50, "wait_ms": 1800000 },
  "search": { "enabled": true, "posts": 284, "indexed": 32, "pending": 251, "failed": 1, "running": true,
              "last_run_at": 1789999700000, "rate_per_hour": 96,
              "next": [ { "post_key": "<sid or legacy key>", "title": "IMG_4021.mp4" } ] },
  "posters": { "pending": 3 } }
```

- **`line`**: exactly the body `GET /studio/line` answers for this key (section 17.4), or `null` when the Durable Object fails,
  answers non-2xx or takes over 5 s. The Worker asks the Durable Object for `GET /studio/line` with the caller's key id, the way it
  proxies that route. The Durable Object answers it from its storage and D1 before any container code runs, so polling `/queue`
  never starts the container. It is the same cost as polling `/studio/line` (which `/queue` replaces on this screen).
- **`search`**: `{ "enabled": false }` alone while search is off (`features.search` false). Otherwise:
  - `posts` = `indexed` + `pending` + `failed`, every live library post (the post key `GET /library` groups by).
  - `indexed`: posts whose vector matches their current inputs.
  - `pending`: posts the next pass would embed (never embedded, inputs changed, or a refusal whose hour is up).
  - `failed`: posts the Space refused that wait, either for their retry hour or for good after three refusals (until their inputs
    change).
  - `next`: up to 5 pending posts in the order they will be embedded, newest first; `title` is what the post is embedded under (custom
    title, else the lead file's name; may be null).
  - `running`: a run (cron or `POST /library/search/index`) has started, has not finished, and started less than 11.5 minutes ago (the
    10-minute budget plus one Space call). A run whose Worker died stops counting after that.
  - `last_run_at`: when the latest run started (ms), or null before the first.
  - `rate_per_hour`: posts embedded by the runs that started in the last hour; null when none started in that hour.
  - `running`, `last_run_at` and `rate_per_hour` are null while migration 0011 is not applied.
- **`posters`**: `{ "pending" }`, the originals that still need a server-made poster and are not in their one-day cooldown after a
  failure (what the poster kick would queue, section 13). Null on a D1 failure.

### 23.2 How the app merges it with its local jobs

- Its own jobs stay the source of truth for what it started: progress comes from the session and render routes as before.
- A line entry or `running` with `mine: true` carries `sid` (and `job` for a render). The app matches it to a local job by `sid` for a
  save and by `sid` + `job` for a render, and shows the entry's `position` on that job ("2nd in line"). A `mine` entry with no local job
  (the share extension, or another device using the same key) is shown as its own row, from `link` and `origin`.
- Entries with `mine: false` are other callers' work: one row each with `key_name` and `kind`, no link. They explain why the app's own
  job waits.
- `search` is one row while `pending` > 0 or `running` is true: "Indexing for search: `indexed` of `posts`", with `next` titles under
  it and, when `rate_per_hour` > 0, an estimate of `pending / rate_per_hour` hours. `failed` > 0 can show as a quiet note. Hidden when
  `enabled` is false.
- `posters` is one row while `pending` > 0.
- A `null` part keeps the last known value for that part and marks it stale; it never removes the app's local jobs.
- Poll while the screen is visible: every 5 s while the line has work or `search.running` is true, else every 30 s. Polling never
  wakes the container.

### 23.3 Indexing cadence

- The cron (`*/15 * * * *`, `src/index.ts`) runs `drainIndexer` (`src/embed.ts`) and awaits it: passes of 8 posts until a stop.
  Stops: `empty` (nothing pending), `unavailable` (the Space is asleep or down), `stalled` (a pass embedded and refused nothing),
  `budget` (10 minutes, `SEARCH_RUN_BUDGET_MS`, would be passed by another pass as long as the last one), `error` (D1 threw). The
  first pass always runs. At ~50 s a batch that is about 12 passes, ~96 posts a run, ~380 an hour, and a run ends before the next
  one starts.
- `POST /library/search/index` is one pass (`passes`/`stop` not in the answer); `?drain=1` is the same loop under 25 s
  (`SEARCH_DRAIN_REQUEST_MS`), so it usually runs one or two passes.
- New posts need no trigger: every pass compares all posts' inputs with their stored hashes, so the next run picks them up.
- Every run is a row in D1 `search_index_runs` (migration `0011_search_runs.sql`): `trigger` (`cron` | `request`), `started_at`,
  `finished_at` (null while running), `passes`, `indexed`, `failed`, `unavailable`, `stop`. It is updated after each pass, so
  `GET /queue` shows progress mid-run. Rows older than 7 days are deleted when a run starts. Without the table the indexer still runs
  and keeps no record.

### 23.4 Capability, files, deploy

`features.queue: true` in `GET /capabilities`. Files: `src/queue.ts` (the route), `src/embed.ts` (`drainIndexer`, `searchQueue`),
`src/poster.ts` (`posterBacklog`), `src/gate.ts` (`queue`), `src/worker.ts`, `src/index.ts`; tests in `test/search.test.ts`. Apply
migration 0011 (additive, one table), then deploy the API Worker. Either order works: without the table `GET /queue` answers null for
the run fields and the cron drains without a record.

## 24. Gallery share links (addendum, built 2026-10-17; owner: "select and then hit a button to create a shareable link with gallery in the rich ... the button can not work if I exceed the limit", "a separate tab for all created gallery links and I can revoke anytime")

A share set is 2..10 public files under one link (2..4 until section 29), `https://share.capybaraharmony.com/g/<id>`. In Discord it shows as one embed with the files in a grid (videos play); other apps show the first file. `<id>` is 19 random digits (Discord's post renderer expects numeric status ids; ~63 bits, unguessable). D1 table `share_sets` (migration `0012_share_sets.sql`).

### 24.1 Routes (keyed `Authorization: Api-Key`; the library service and other methods 404)

- `POST /share/sets` body `{ "items": ["<library file id>", ...], "title"?: "<1..80 chars>" }` → 201 `{ "status": "success", "set": <set> }`. `items` is 2..10 distinct ids (`share_set_max`), in the order shown. Each must be a live file that is public now (the same rows the library calls `visibility: "public"` with a `url`). Errors: 400 `error.share.bad_request` (not 2..10, duplicates, bad shape, title too long); 409 `error.share.not_public` with `"items": [<ids that are missing or private>]`; 503 `error.api.generic`.
- `GET /share/sets` → 200 `{ "status": "success", "sets": [<set>, ...] }`, live sets only, newest first, at most 200.
- `DELETE /share/sets/<id>` → 200 `{ "status": "success" }` (revoked: the link is a 404 at once; repeat is 200). Unknown id 404 `error.share.not_found`.

`<set>` = `{ "id", "url": "<share_base_url>g/<id>", "title": string|null, "created_at": <ms>, "items": [ { "id", "url": <public file URL, or null when it is no longer public>, "content_type", "poster_url", "width", "height", "duration" } ] }`. A set whose files all went private or were deleted stays listed (its page is a 404) until revoked.

`GET /capabilities`: `features.share_sets` is true exactly while `share_base_url` is non-null; `limits.share_set_max` is 10 (4 before section 29; the app reads it, so no app change was needed).

### 24.2 The page (`share.` host, no key)

- `GET|HEAD /g/<id>`: Open Graph like section 22, built from the set's files that are still public, in order (none left, revoked or unknown: the same 404). The first video is `og:video`; every image, webp and video poster is an `og:image`. For Discord's crawler only (user agent contains `Discordbot`), the head also carries a component embed (https://docs.discord.com/developers/link-previews/component-embeds): `<script id="discord:component-embed" type="application/json">` with `{"component":{"type":17,"components":[{"type":10,"content":<title or "N files", markdown-escaped>},{"type":12,"items":[{"media":{"url":<file url>}},...]}]}}` (under 3,000 bytes, `<` escaped). Discord shows every file in one media gallery, videos with play controls; the Open Graph tags are its fallback.
- `GET /api/v1/statuses/<id>` and `GET /users/cobalt/statuses/<id>`: a Mastodon v1 Status (`id`, `url`, `uri`, `created_at`, `content` = the escaped title, `visibility: "public"`, an `account` named cobalt, `media_attachments` with `type` image / gifv / video, `url`, `preview_url`, `meta.original` width/height). Videos over 1920 px on a side report half their size (Discord refuses larger). Same 404 rules.
- `GET /owoembed?id=<id>` → oEmbed `{ "version": "1.0", "type": "rich", "title": "Embed", "provider_name": "cobalt", "provider_url": "<share_base_url>", "author_name": <title or "N files"> }`.
- The status and oEmbed routes are Discord's undocumented Mastodon path. They still answer, but the page no longer links them: Discord showed only thumbnails through it.

### 24.3 App

The library gets a select mode; with 2..4 public files selected, `make share link` creates a set and copies its link; with more than 4, or any private file, the button is off and says why. A `links` tab (shown only with `features.share_sets`) lists the sets (thumbnails, title, created date, copy, revoke with a confirm). No migration of anything existing. Deploy: apply 0012, then the API Worker.

## 25. Roast captions (addendum, built 2026-10-18; owner: "use haiku ... to adjust the title and description of the rich embed ... set once for all media and share ... funny and why Harmony the capybara chose this content with emojis, more of a roast of me")

- Claude Haiku 5.5 (`claude-haiku-5-5`, `ROAST_MODEL`) writes one title (at most 100 code points) and description (at most 280) per public file and per live gallery link, in the voice of Harmony the capybara roasting Harmony, with emojis. It reads the post's title or file name, where it was saved from, the duration, and one still per file (a video's poster, an image itself; jpeg/png/gif/webp only). It never downloads a video and never sees an embedding.
- Stored once in D1 `media_captions` (migration 0013), keyed `item:<media_items id>` or `set:<share_sets id>`; previews never wait on the model. A failure stores `title NULL` and is retried after a day, at most 3 attempts; a row with nothing to caption is marked as used up.
- When: a new gallery link at `POST /share/sets` (the Worker waits at most 6 s, its own timeout; a miss is left to the cron), everything else from the 15-minute cron (gallery links first, then public files newest first, up to 90 s a run, before the search indexer).
- Where: single-file pages (section 22) use the caption as `og:title` unless the post has a title set in the app, and its description as `og:description`/`twitter:description` (followed by the video length). Gallery pages (section 24) use the link's own title if Harmony typed one, else the caption's, and its description; Discord's component embed shows `**title**` then the description above the gallery.
- Revised 2026-10-18 (owner: "just make it one sentence summary of all the content ... you dont have to be a capybara ... a cute furry cat girl ... don't announce urself", "furry anime girl ascii emoji cute funny", "the ai summary can just be the title ... not additional description", "update the existing rich embeds too"): the caption is now ONE sentence (at most 160 code points) summing up all the content, in a cute, funny furry anime cat-girl voice with kaomoji text faces such as (=^･ω･^=) or >w<, never emoji (removed on save), never introducing or naming itself. It is the title only: the description is stored empty and ignored, so pages show `og:description` as before captions (the video length) and Discord's component embed shows just `**title**` above the gallery. The stored `model` carries the caption style (`claude-haiku-5-5+v3-kaomoji-catgirl`); the cron rewrites every caption written in an older style, gallery links first, then files newest first, and an older caption stays up until its own is rewritten.
- Length (2026-10-18, after the first rewrite cut sentences off): the model is asked for at most 110 characters, kaomoji included; a sentence over 140 is asked for again once ("write it again in at most 110"), and only a second long answer is cut at a word boundary with "..." and its closing kaomoji kept. Style `v4-kaomoji-catgirl-short`: captions of the previous style (`v3-kaomoji-catgirl`) are rewritten only when they contain "..." (were cut); every older one is rewritten.
- Off unless `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is in the secrets file; optional `ROAST_BASE_URL` (secret) sends calls through Cloudflare AI Gateway (`https://gateway.ai.cloudflare.com/v1/<account>/<gateway>/anthropic`). No app change; no new route.

## 26. Share link collections (addendum, built 2026-10-18; owner: "CRUD collections which have shares in them")

A collection is a named folder of gallery links (section 24). Keyed routes only (a service credential gets 404), D1 only, `cache-control: no-store`, 404 `error.share.off` while the share host is not configured. `GET /capabilities` has `features.share_collections` (true exactly with `share_sets`).

Shape: `{ "id": "<16 letters/digits>", "name": "memes", "created_at": <ms>, "updated_at": <ms>, "set_ids": ["<19-digit set id>", ...] }`. `set_ids` lists the collection's live links, most recently added first; a revoked link drops out of every collection on its own. A link may be in several collections. The app joins `set_ids` with `GET /share/sets` for thumbnails and URLs.

- `GET /share/collections` → `200 { "status": "success", "collections": [shape, ...] }`, most recently changed first (at most 100).
- `POST /share/collections` `{ "name": "memes", "set_ids": [...]? }` → `201 { "status": "success", "collection": shape }`. Name: trimmed, control characters removed, 1..60 code points. `set_ids` optional, at most 200, live links only.
- `PATCH /share/collections/<id>` `{ "name"?: "...", "add"?: [...], "remove"?: [...] }` (at least one) → `200 { "status": "success", "collection": shape }`. Adding a link already there is a no-op; removing one that is not there is a no-op. Bumps `updated_at`.
- `DELETE /share/collections/<id>` → `200 { "status": "success" }`. Deletes the folder only: its links stay live and keep working.
- Errors: `400 error.collection.bad_request` (bad JSON, name or id list), `404 error.collection.not_found` (unknown collection), `409 error.collection.link_not_found` with `set_ids` (unknown or revoked links in `set_ids`/`add`; nothing changed), `409 error.collection.too_many` (100 collections), `409 error.collection.full` (200 links), `503 error.api.generic`.
- `POST /share/collections/<id>/reroll` (owner: "reroll links in a collection all at once", "Link urls which will be the title too") → `200 { "status": "success", "collection": shape, "replaced": { "<old set id>": "<new set id>", ... } }`. Every live link in the collection gets a new URL: a new share set with the same files and no typed title (so its title is a fresh caption, section 25, written within about 8 s, the rest by the cron); the old link is revoked (its page is a 404 at once) and every collection that held it holds the new one in the same place. `404 error.collection.not_found`, `409 error.collection.empty` (no live links). The app refetches `GET /share/sets` afterwards and confirms first ("the old links stop working"). Discord caches previews per URL, so a reroll is how a link gets a fresh preview there.
- App: in the Links tab, collections as folders: create (name, optionally with the selected links), rename, delete (confirm: "delete this collection? its links keep working"), add a link to collections from its row, remove it from one. Migration 0014 must be applied before the API deploy that ships these routes.

## 27. Chat (addendum, pinned 2026-10-18; owner: "an ai for creating threads and talking about files", "Haiku, chat (tools look at the data and run its own sandbox and use tools as code in sandbox)")

Threads with Claude Haiku 5.5 (`claude-haiku-5-5`, `CHAT_MODEL`) about the library. The model has one tool, `run_code`: it writes JavaScript that calls the library tools below, and the Worker runs that code in a fresh Cloudflare Dynamic Worker (Worker Loader binding `LOADER`, "code mode"): no network (`globalOutbound: null`), 30 s wall, 5,000 ms CPU, the tools reached only over RPC, read-only except `show`/`look`. D1 tables `chat_threads`, `chat_messages` (migration 0015). Keyed routes only (a service credential gets 404), `cache-control: no-store`. `GET /capabilities`: `features.chat` is true exactly while `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set and the Worker has `LOADER`; otherwise every `/chat` route answers 404 `error.chat.off`.

### 27.1 Shapes

- `<thread>` = `{ "id": "<16 letters/digits>", "title": "cat clips from october", "created_at": <ms>, "updated_at": <ms>, "preview": "<first 120 chars of the last message's text>", "message_count": <n> }`. A new thread is titled from its first message (trimmed, 60 code points); after the first reply the model renames it (at most 60) unless it was renamed by hand.
- `<message>` = `{ "id": "<number as string>", "role": "user"|"assistant", "text": "<markdown>", "post_ids": [<library post ids>], "steps": [<step>], "status": "done"|"running"|"error", "error": null|"<code>", "created_at": <ms> }`.
  - `post_ids`: on a user message, the files the user attached ("ask about this", at most 20); on an assistant message, the files the model chose to show (at most 12), in order. Post ids are the `GET /library` post `id`s.
  - `<step>` = `{ "id": "<tool_use id>", "kind": "code", "status": "running"|"done"|"error", "code": "<the JavaScript, up to 8,000 chars>", "logs": ["..."], "result": "<JSON preview, up to 2,000 chars>"|null, "error": null|"<message>", "duration_ms": <n>|null }`. The app shows steps collapsed ("ran code · 3 tools · 0.8 s"), expandable to code, logs, result.
  - `status: "running"` means a reply is still being written (or the stream broke: a message still running after 3 minutes is answered as `error: "error.chat.interrupted"`).
- `posts`: thread reads carry `"posts": { "<post id>": <GET /library?v=3 post shape>, ... }` for every post any message references and that still exists. A deleted post is missing from the map; the app shows a "removed" placeholder.

### 27.2 Routes

- `GET /chat/threads` → `200 { "status": "success", "threads": [<thread>, ...] }`, most recently updated first, at most 200.
- `POST /chat/threads` `{ "title"?: "..." }` → `201 { "status": "success", "thread": <thread> }`. Creates an empty thread (titled "new chat" until its first message); "ask about this" sends the post ids with the first message. At most 200 threads (`409 error.chat.too_many`).
- `GET /chat/threads/<id>` → `200 { "status": "success", "thread": <thread>, "messages": [<message>, ...], "posts": {...} }`, oldest first, all messages (at most 200 per thread).
- `PATCH /chat/threads/<id>` `{ "title": "..." }` (1..60 code points after trim) → `200 { "status": "success", "thread": <thread> }`.
- `DELETE /chat/threads/<id>` → `200 { "status": "success" }` (its messages too). Unknown: 404 `error.chat.not_found`.
- `POST /chat/threads/<id>/messages` `{ "text": "<1..4000 chars>", "post_ids"?: [<at most 20>] }` → `200 text/event-stream`. One reply at a time per thread: while the last assistant message is running, `409 error.chat.busy`. Unknown post ids are dropped silently. 200 messages in the thread: `409 error.chat.full`. Events, each `event: <name>\ndata: <one JSON line>\n\n`:
  - `user` `{ "message": <message> }`: the saved user message (first event).
  - `assistant` `{ "message": <message> }`: the assistant message row, `status: "running"`, empty text.
  - `text` `{ "delta": "..." }`: text to append, in order.
  - `step` `{ "step": <step> }`: a code step started (`running`) or finished (`done`/`error`); the same `id` again replaces the earlier one.
  - `posts` `{ "post_ids": [...], "posts": {...} }`: files the model chose to show, appended to the message's `post_ids`, with their shapes.
  - `title` `{ "thread": <thread> }`: the model renamed the thread (after the first reply only).
  - `done` `{ "message": <message>, "thread": <thread> }`: the final, saved assistant message. Last event.
  - `error` `{ "code": "error.chat.model"|"error.chat.interrupted"|"error.api.generic", "message": <message>|null }`: the reply failed; the message is saved with `status: "error"` and whatever text it had. Last event.
  - `: ping` comment lines every 15 s keep the connection open.
  If the stream drops, the reply keeps going server-side for as long as the Worker lets it; the app re-reads `GET /chat/threads/<id>` (every 2 s while the last message is `running`, at most 3 minutes).
- Errors: `400 error.chat.bad_request` (bad JSON, empty or long text, bad ids), `404 error.chat.not_found`, `404 error.chat.off`, `409 error.chat.busy|full|too_many`, `503 error.api.generic`.

### 27.3 The sandbox tools (what the model's code can call, all async, on the global `cobalt`)

- `cobalt.library({ since?, until?, source?, kind?, query?, limit? })` → posts newest first (default 100, at most 1,000). `since`/`until` ISO dates or ms; `source` a host such as `"tiktok.com"`; `kind` `"video"|"image"|"gallery"`; `query` plain substring of title/name/link. Each `{ id, title, saved_at (ISO), source, link, kind, files, duration, public, caption }` (`caption` the roast caption title, or null).
- `cobalt.search(query, limit?)` → the meaning search (section 21), best first, at most 500 (default 10), the same records plus `score`. Throws "search is waking up" while the Space sleeps.
- `cobalt.post(id)` → one record plus `files: [{ id, name, kind, content_type, duration, width, height, bytes, public, url, share_url }]`.
- `cobalt.look(ids)` → the model sees up to 4 pictures (posters or images) after the code returns. Returns how many were queued.
- `cobalt.show(ids)` → shows up to 12 files in the reply as thumbnails; returns the ids accepted.
- `cobalt.links()` → gallery share links `[{ id, url, title, created_at, post_ids }]`; `cobalt.collections()` → `[{ id, name, link_ids }]`.
- At most 8 code runs per reply, 50 tool calls per run, results over 20,000 chars are cut for the model.
- Since section 33 these are ops in the registry, called as `cobalt.library.list`, `cobalt.library.search`, `cobalt.library.post`, `cobalt.links.list` and `cobalt.collections.list` (the flat names above still work).

### 27.4 App (apple/CONTRACT.md)

Tabs become save · library · links · chat · settings (`chat` only with `features.chat`). Queue moves to a toolbar button with the in-flight badge on save and library (and keeps its sidebar row on the Mac). The chat tab: a thread list (new, rename, delete with confirm), a thread view streaming the reply, code steps collapsed, shown files as thumbnails that open the post. "ask about this" on a post starts a new thread with that post attached. Deploy: apply 0015, then the API Worker (needs the `LOADER` binding).


### 27.5 Collages (addendum, 2026-10-18; owner: "if I search for cats, there could be like 500 items ... a mini masonry like collage of a search that can paginate through. And when you hold press, it shows the preview")

- Sandbox tool `cobalt.collage(ids, { title?, query? })` → attaches ONE big result set to the reply: up to 500 post ids in order (unknown ids dropped), `title` (≤80, e.g. "every cat"), `query` (≤200, the search words, for "open in library"). A later call in the same reply replaces it; returns how many ids were kept. `show()` stays for a few highlights (≤12).
- `<message>` gains `"collage": null | { "title": string|null, "query": string|null, "total": <n> }` (assistant messages; always null on user messages). D1: `chat_messages.collage` (migration 0016).
- SSE event `collage` `{ "collage": {title, query, total} }` after the run that set it; `done` carries it on the message too.
- `GET /chat/threads/<id>/messages/<message id>/collage?offset=<0..>&limit=<1..60, default 30>` (keyed) → `200 { "status": "success", "title", "query", "total": <n>, "offset": <n>, "next": <offset of the next page>|null, "post_ids": [...], "posts": { "<post id>": <GET /library?v=3 post shape> } }`. `post_ids` is the page in order, minus posts deleted since (so a page can be shorter than `limit`; `total` does not change). No collage on that message: 404 `error.chat.not_found`; bad offset/limit: 400 `error.chat.bad_request`.
- App: under the reply text, a masonry collage (2 columns on iPhone, more on iPad/Mac) of the first page, with the title and "N results", a "more" control that loads the next page, tap opens the post, long-press shows a context-menu preview (poster or image, title) with open/share actions, and "open in library" runs `query` as a library search (shown only when `query` is set).
- Deploy: apply 0016, then the API Worker.
### 27.9 Chat replies on the owner's iPhone (addendum, 2026-10-28; owner: "support apple shortcut for also calling the ai and inputting a msg along with live activity for agent activity", "if i use mac ... it shows up on ios?")

A reply's Live Activity (`CobaltChatActivityAttributes` in the app: `thread`, `prompt`, `source`; content state below)
shows what the AI is doing. The app draws it itself for a message it sent (the chat tab, the "Ask chat" Shortcut,
apple/CONTRACT-PARALLEL.md 15.10). A message sent from another device (the Mac app, a Mac Shortcut) reaches the iPhone
through APNs, with the same APNs secrets as section 8 (`src/chat-live.ts`).

- `features.chat_live_push`: `chat` and APNs configured.
- `PUT /live/chat-start-token` `{"token": "<hex>", "environment": "sandbox"|"production"}` (keyed; `DELETE` forgets it): the
  device's push-to-start token for chat activities. `200 {"pushing": true}`; `{"pushing": false}` without APNs.
- `PUT /live/chats/<thread>` (keyed, same body): the update token of an activity the server push-started on this device.
- The chat route hands the reply's state to the `main` Durable Object (`POST /live/chats/<thread>/state`, internal key,
  never public): at its start, at once on a status change (a tool call starts or ends, files shown, done, failed), and
  more text at most every 3 s. On a new reply the DO push-starts the activity (`attributes.source: "other"`, alert
  "chat is thinking") on every device with a start token **except the sender's** (the sender draws its own), then updates
  it once that device has sent its update token (text at most every 5 s, priority 5; anything else priority 10) and ends
  it with the reply (alert "chat replied" + the start of the reply, dismissed after 10 min; failed: 5 min). A reply with no
  device to show it stops handing over after the first call.
- Content state (Swift property names; `steps`, `failedSteps`, `shown` always present): `status` `thinking` | `working` |
  `writing` | `done` | `failed`, `since` (unix s, the reply's start), `steps` (code runs started), `failedSteps`,
  `tool?` (what the running or last code run does, from its `cobalt.<tool>(` calls: "searching your library"; a tool added
  later reads "using <name>"), `reply?` (plain text: the last 160 characters while writing, the first 160 once done),
  `shown` (files shown plus the collage's size), `code?` (failed).
- Not done: no push while the iPhone itself is the sender and the app is suspended mid-reply (its own activity then goes
  stale until the app runs again).

## 28. Collection sets (addendum, built 2026-10-19; owner: "a new primitive called sets which has groups of collections")

A set is a named group of collections (section 26). Internally it is a "collection group" everywhere (D1 `collection_groups`, `src/collection-groups.ts`, routes `/share/groups`, Swift `CollectionGroup`), because "share set" already means a gallery link (section 24); only the app's copy says "set". Keyed routes only (a service credential gets 404), D1 only, `cache-control: no-store`, 404 `error.share.off` while the share host is not configured. `GET /capabilities` has `features.share_groups` (true exactly with `share_collections`).

Shape: `{ "id": "<16 letters/digits>", "name": "weekend", "created_at": <ms>, "updated_at": <ms>, "collection_ids": ["<16-char collection id>", ...] }`. `collection_ids` lists the group's existing collections, most recently added first; a deleted collection drops out of every group (its membership rows are removed with it, and the listing joins on `share_collections` regardless). A collection may be in several groups. The app joins `collection_ids` with `GET /share/collections`.

- `GET /share/groups` → `200 { "status": "success", "groups": [shape, ...] }`, most recently changed first (at most 100).
- `POST /share/groups` `{ "name": "weekend", "collection_ids": [...]? }` → `201 { "status": "success", "group": shape }`. Name: same rules as a collection (trimmed, control characters removed, 1..60 code points). `collection_ids` optional, at most 100, existing collections only.
- `PATCH /share/groups/<id>` `{ "name"?: "...", "add"?: [...], "remove"?: [...] }` (at least one) → `200 { "status": "success", "group": shape }`. Adding a collection already there is a no-op; removing one that is not there is a no-op. Bumps `updated_at`.
- `DELETE /share/groups/<id>` → `200 { "status": "success" }`. Deletes the group only: its collections and their links stay.
- Errors: `400 error.group.bad_request` (bad JSON, name or id list), `404 error.group.not_found` (unknown group), `409 error.group.collection_not_found` with `collection_ids` (unknown collections in `collection_ids`/`add`; nothing changed), `409 error.group.too_many` (100 groups), `409 error.group.full` (100 collections), `503 error.api.generic`.
- No reroll and no server-side copy text: the app builds "copy all links" itself (CobaltKit `ShareSetStore.linksText(in: CollectionGroup)`): every live link URL of the group's collections, one per line, in the group's order then each collection's order, a link held by two of its collections once.
- App: sets are shown only when `features.share_groups` is true: create (name, optionally with collections), rename, delete (confirm: "delete this set? its collections keep their links"), add a collection to sets, remove it from one. Migration 0017 must be applied before the API deploy that ships these routes.

## 29. Gallery links that move: 10 files and moving previews (addendum, built 2026-10-19; owner: "can we do D with autoplay?", D = a 10-file gallery)

Discord's media gallery shows up to 10 files, and it plays animated images (GIF, WebP) by itself while a video always waits for a tap. So a gallery link now holds up to 10 files, and each public video in a live link gets a moving preview: a short, silent animated WebP that Discord's gallery shows in its place.

- **Limit**: `SHARE_SET_MAX` 10, `limits.share_set_max` 10. Ten media URLs are about 700 bytes, well inside the 3,000-byte component embed limit; over it the description is dropped, then the button, then the component embed (Open Graph alone).
- **The preview**: the video's first 4 s (its whole length when shorter; none under 0.5 s), 480 px wide (320 when the video is narrower), 12 fps, quality `low`. Made by the helper's ordinary webp job (`POST /jobs/upload`, owner label `loop:<item id>`) from the stored file (the private original, or the public copy when that is the only one), so the line sees it as a running job, the job sweep collects it into the PUBLIC bucket as `<10 base62>.webp`, and no library row is written. One at a time, only while nothing else needs the helper, like posters. D1 `media_loops` (migration 0018): `item_id`, `url`, `r2_key`, `bytes`, `tried_at`, `made_at`. A file the helper refuses, or a third failure, waits a day.
- **Queued**: `POST /share/sets` with a video asks the Durable Object (`POST /loops/kick`, internal) to queue the link's videos, through `waitUntil`; the daily cron kicks again (`?limit=50&clean=1`) for anything missed and deletes the previews of videos that were deleted or made private (object and row).
- **The page** (`GET /g/<id>`, Discord's crawler only): in the component embed's Media Gallery a video with a preview is that WebP; when any is, the container ends with an Action Row holding one link Button (style 5) `watch with sound` to the page itself, where the videos play with controls. Open Graph is unchanged (the first video stays `og:video`), so every other app is as before. Without the table (0018 not applied) the page is exactly what it was.
- **Not changed**: the app (it follows `limits.share_set_max`), the helper and the container image, the Mastodon status and oEmbed routes.
- Deploy: apply 0018, then the API Worker. Check one link in Discord: the gallery should move without a tap.

### 29.1 Moving previews off by default (2026-10-20; owner: "I don't need webp", picked "real videos back")

The swap above cost too much: every video in Discord became a 4 s, silent, 480 px WebP. The API Worker var
`GALLERY_LOOPS` now decides it: `"1"` is everything above; anything else, the default (`"0"` in
`cloudflare.config.ts`), puts every file in the Media Gallery as itself (videos are the real file, full quality with
sound, played with a tap), drops the `watch with sound` button, and queues no previews (neither at link creation nor
in the daily cron). Existing previews and `media_loops` rows stay in place, unused, so turning it back on is the var
alone.

## 30. Stage: the stream canvas, the viewer store and Twitch channel points (addendum, pinned 2026-10-19; owner: "a presentation mode where I can randomly cycle through my media content ... all over the canvas which will be 1080p", "a studio part of the software both macos and ios where I can also have twitch viewers submit points", "a toggle for the store which just locks it entirely not even able to login")

Approved mockup: https://claude.ai/artifact/ANXn8CohVKhHrGWuuW1Tsd (boards: stream canvas, Mac studio, iPhone studio, store).
Owner decisions (2026-10-19 thread): the canvas is a web page at a URL with a secret token that OBS loads as a
Browser Source, and the token can be rotated; tiles land at random spots and sizes and overlap, with a "how crazy"
slider from 5 to 100 tiles on screen; the pool is a collection, a set, a search or the whole library; one spotlight
tile at a time is big and has sound; viewers spend real Twitch channel points on summon, chaos, shuffle and pin, with
no approval step; viewer-submitted links are out; viewers can sign in with Twitch on the store page, client side only;
the store has a lock that turns the whole store off, sign-in included.

**Naming.** "studio" already means the save/trim session everywhere in this codebase (`/studio/<sid>`,
`STUDIO-CONTRACT.md`, `studio.ts`). This feature is **stage** in code, routes, D1 tables and Swift types
(`stage.ts`, `/stage`, `stage_*`, `Stage*`). Only user-facing copy says "studio": the app tab, and the host
`studio.capybaraharmony.com`.

### 30.1 Hosts, config, capability

- New Worker domain `studio.capybaraharmony.com` on the API Worker (`cloudflare.config.ts` `domains`), plain var
  `STAGE_BASE_URL = "https://studio.capybaraharmony.com/"`. Like the share host, the Worker answers that host itself,
  before the gate, never wakes the container, sends no CORS, and serves only the paths in 30.4 (anything else 404).
- Secrets (all from `~/.config/cobalt/secrets.json`; `cf deploy` refuses while a declared one is missing, so the
  owner adds them first, empty is fine): `TWITCH_CLIENT_ID`, `TWITCH_CLIENT_SECRET`, `TWITCH_EVENTSUB_SECRET`
  (10..100 ASCII chars, made by the owner, e.g. `openssl rand -hex 32`).
- `GET /capabilities`: `features.stage` is true exactly while `STAGE_BASE_URL` is an https URL (canvas and store work
  without Twitch). `features.stage_twitch` is true exactly with `stage` and all three Twitch secrets non-empty.
  With `stage` false every `/stage*` route answers 404 `error.stage.off` and the stage host answers 404 to everything.
  Any request to a `studio.*` host that is not the configured stage host (stage off, or another name) is a plain 404
  before the gate: it never reaches the API's routes.
- New Durable Object class `StageRoom` (binding `STAGE_ROOM`, sqlite storage, one instance `idFromName("main")`):
  holds the canvas WebSockets (hibernation API) and fans out events (30.6). Imported only by `index.ts`, like
  `ModelRelay`.
- D1 migration `0019_stage.sql` (owner applies it before the API deploy): tables in 30.2. Additive only.

### 30.2 D1 (migration 0019)

- `stage_settings`: exactly one row, `id = 'main'`, created on first read with the defaults below. Columns:
  `on_air` (0/1, default 0), `pool_kind` (`'library'|'collection'|'group'|'search'`, default `'library'`),
  `pool_ref` (collection or group id, or the search words; NULL for library), `pool_ids` (JSON array of
  `media_items` ids: the resolved pool, see 30.3), `pool_at` (ms), `density` (5..100, default 30), `tile_seconds`
  (3..120, default 10; was 3..30, see 30.12), `tile_scale` (0.5..1.6, default 1.0), `spotlight_auto` (0/1, default 1),
  `spotlight_seconds` (10..120, default 20), `sound` (0/1, default 1), `video_max` (0..24, default 12),
  `store_open` (0/1, default 0: the store starts locked), `store_hint` (0/1, default 1), `live_token` (32 random
  base62 chars, made on first read), `oauth_state`, `oauth_state_at`, `updated_at`.
- `stage_twitch`: at most one row, `id = 'main'`: `broadcaster_id`, `login`, `display_name`, `access_token`,
  `refresh_token` (both AES-GCM encrypted, key = HKDF-SHA-256 of `TWITCH_CLIENT_SECRET` with info
  `"stage-twitch-tokens"`, stored `base64(iv).base64(ciphertext)`), `expires_at` (ms), `scopes`, `eventsub_id`,
  `connected_at`, `last_event_at`.
- `stage_rewards`: one row per action (`summon`, `chaos`, `shuffle`, `pin`): `action` PK, `twitch_reward_id`,
  `title`, `prompt`, `cost`, `cooldown_seconds`, `enabled` (0/1), `updated_at`. Seeded on first read with:
  summon 500 / 30 s / on, chaos 2000 / 300 s / on, shuffle 1000 / 120 s / on, pin 300 / 60 s / off.
  Titles: `summon`, `chaos`, `shuffle`, `pin` prefixed with `capystore ` on Twitch only if the bare word is taken
  (Twitch reward titles are unique per channel). Summon's prompt: `"a code from the store, or a few words"`.
- `stage_redemptions`: `id` (the Twitch redemption id, or `owner:<16 base62>` for the owner's own actions) PK,
  `action`, `user_id`, `user_login`, `user_name`, `input` (≤200), `item_id` (NULL unless a summon resolved),
  `status` (`'done'|'refunded'|'failed'`), `reason` (NULL or `'off_air'|'no_match'|'no_effect'|'disabled'|'error'`),
  `created_at`. Index `(created_at DESC)` and `(user_id, created_at DESC)`. Rows older than 30 days are deleted by
  the daily cron.
- Built additions (all additive): `stage_twitch.status`/`status_at` (where `reconnect`/`not_affiliate` live),
  `stage_rewards.synced`, and `stage_seen` (EventSub message ids for the 10-minute dedupe); redemption ids are
  unique, so a redelivered redemption never runs twice.
- `stage_codes`: `code` PK (3 chars from `ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789`, upper case), `item_id` UNIQUE,
  `created_at`. A pool item gets a code the first time the pool is resolved with it in; codes never change and are
  matched case-insensitively. When 3-char codes run low (more than half used) new ones are 4 chars.

### 30.3 The pool

The pool is a list of **public** library files (images, GIFs, videos, WebPs): a file is in it only while it is
public (section 16: a file the library reports with `public: true` and a public `url`); private files never reach
the canvas, the store or a summon. Deleted files drop out. Resolution:

- `library`: the newest 2,000 public files.
- `collection` (`pool_ref` = a share collection id, section 26): every file of every live gallery link in it, in
  the collection's order.
- `group` (`pool_ref` = a collection group id, section 28): every file of every collection in it.
- `search` (`pool_ref` = the words): the meaning search (section 21) over posts, best first, at most 200 posts,
  then their public files; posts scoring under `SEARCH_MIN_SCORE` (0.2, `stage.ts`, to tune against the real
